New Delhi, the 31st October, 1968 
S .O . 3944. — In pursuance of Section 17 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 ol 
1937 ) , the Central Government hereby publishes the following award of the Central 
Industrial Tribunal, Delhi in the industrial dispute between the employers in relation to 
the Punjab National Bank Limited and their workmen , which was received by the Central 
Government on tho 18th October, 1968. 

BEFORE THE CENTRAL GOVT. INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL, DELHI. 
PRESENT : 
Shri R . K . Baweja , Central Govt. Industrial Tribunal Delhi. 

11th October, 1968, 
ReFERENCE I. D . No. 1 OF 1968 , 

BETWEEN 
The employers in relation to the Punjab National Bank Ltd ., 


-AND 


Their workmen . 
Shri M . K . Jain - for the hunk , 
Shri C L . Bhardwaj - for the workman , 

AWARD 
By Order No . 51/ 75 /67 / LRIII , dated 4th January , 1968 , the Government of India 
referred to this Tribunal for adjudication the following industrial dispute between the 
aforosaid parties : 
" Whether the action of the management of the Punjab National Bank Ltd . in 

terminating the services of Shri Dharambir Sharma, Daftry at the Burham 
pur Branch of the Bank , on the 22nd Junc, 1966 is justified ? If not, to what 

relief is he entitled ? " 
2 . The facts of this case as alleged in the statement of claim fled on behalf of the 
workman by the general secretary of All India Punjab Nationol Bank Employccy Associa 
tion . Chandni Chowk , Delhi (hereinafter to be referred as the Association ) , are that the 


- - - - - 


. c. 3(1 ) ] 1 GALLI TE OF INDIA : NOVEMBER 9, 1968 /KARTIKA 18, 1890 5029 

- - 5 - - - 
concerned workman was appointed as a probationer peon- cum -daftry with effect from 21st 
of March , 1966 at thọ Burhampur bran . h of the Punjab National Bank Limited ( herein 
after to ho referred as Bank ) . On the 22nd of June, 1966 his serviçcs were terminated 
with effect from that date on the ground that he was still a minor and was not eligible for 
service of the bank . It is alleged that this was contrary to the provisions of the bank 
award and also against the principles of natural justice inasmuch as the employee was not 
served with a charge -sheet and a notice . 11c IT manager of the bank was approached 
by various reprcsentations but to 10 cffet. Ine workman was , however , taken in service 
with cffect from 21st of September , 1966 as it fresh appointce on the same post. The 
prayer of the Association is that all the termination of services of the workman was 
arbitrary and illegal, he should be treatcl as in service during the interventing period i. e . 
from 22nd of June, 1966 to 20th of September, 1966 and that ho be awarded full wages 
with all other benefits for this period . 


3 . The bank in its written statement raised the preliminary objection that the dispute 
was not an industrial disputo but an individual dispute inasmuch as it had not been raisea 
by a substantial number of workmen . On merits , it was stated that the concerned work 
man submitted an application on the 21st of March , 1966 to tho manager of Khandwa 
office of the bank for appointment as daftry - cum - peon . In the aforesaid application , he 
stated that he was a young man of 19 years and so he was appointed with cicct from that 
date at Burhampur office of the bank . After sometime it transpired that the date of birth 
of the workman was " 20th August , 1948" and on the date of appointment he was a minor 
which was against the rules of the bank . Since he obtained appointment tiy mis -stuleinent 
of facts with regard to his age, his services were rightly terminated on the 22nd of June, 
1966 . It was further stated that on the 26th of June, 1966 the Association by its letter 
dated 26th of June, 1966 brought to the bank s notice that the termination of services of 
the workman was in violation of Para , 139 of tho Sastry Award but this plea was not 
sustainable . Tho bank was further informed by the Association in August, 1966 that on 
an enquiry from the school concerned it had been found that the date of birth mentioned 
in the character certificate issued to the workman was wrongly typed as " 20th August, 
1948 " and the Association requested for reconsideration of the matter. Thereafter and by 
tho time the workman became major, he was appointed afresh on the 21st of September, 
1966 . It was , therefore , pleaded that the workman had no casc and the order of termina 
tion of his services was justificd and that he was not entitled to any relief. 


4 . The preliminary objection regarding the competency of the reference was not pressed 
before me and rightly so . Otherwise allo under Section 2 - A of the industri il Disputes 
Act. 1947 an individual workman can raio 1 dispute regariling the termin . tic .l of his 
services . In the natant case, however , 12 Ast on of which thc workm ) 1 is a mem 
ber bis rainc . this louand on lychalf of the woman and the icfcicnce is pericuíly valid , 


5 . The fact of this wou : nere or salaritic w I ll Dic . onlly now . The 
concora si wokin ritm - " ir i ! ! 17 13 213 ! Murch , 1065 Anitt - er of 
De Punjab Bauonal Bak Limited , Khandwa (Tat. M / 2 ), in which he stated that he was 
a younprin of 11 ve. . Along with that a 120 tion he will ichel a CC , D irm ine 
piincipal of the Goveun. cat Boys Higher Secondary School, Chirag Dollii in which the 
suid principal certifical 1 ) 1 Dharanvir Sh 11 had passed the 10th class cx t , hold 
in April 1964 and that his dute of birth was 20th of August, 1948. It is therefore , 
obvious that though in the application the concerned workman stated til at llace was 19 
ycars , yet in fact, according the certificate att: hed to th : application, hic w . below 18 
at that time. Had there been any mala fide intention on the part of the concerne,l workman 
to conceul his age , he should not have attached that certificate . It is true that in the 
application he made it wrong statement of lucl about his le but that does not seem to be 
milla fide . The manuger or the concerned branch of the bank also due to an over -sight or 
otherwi c on the basis of this application gave him a lotter of ppointment consiilering that 
his agc was 19 years . So it was a bona fide mistake on the part of both the partic , Subsc 
quently , it seems, that the error was detected and by an order datçıl 22nd of 1.103, 1966 
his services were terninted on the ground of his minority vide Ext. M / 3 . It was contend 
ed on behalf of the workina. 1 int if once a person was taken in the employment of the 
bank though he was a minoi, se s SCTVICCS could not be d spensçd with , particularly when 
on the date of toimiantion of his scivicce he had come of ilge as in the present case . On 
the 22nd of June , 1966 , howcver, accor ling to the certificate attached to the application 
he had not come of age as his date of birth as statal therein was 20th of August, 1948 . 
It was only in August, 1966 that the union informed the bank that in fact the date of birth 
of the workman was 20th of June, 1948 and not 20th of Aligust, 1948 as mentioned in the 
certificate . From the school leaving certificate Ext. W / 12 duly proved by Shri D . S . 
Gullaiya , a teacher of the concemed school, it is obvious that the date of birth of the 
employee was 20th of Junc, 1948. But the fict remains that on the date of the termina 
tion of his services neither tho bank nor the union knew that his date of birth was 20th of 
June, 1948 and not the 20th of August, 1948 as furnished by the principal of the school, 


5030 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : NOVEMBER 9, 1968 /KARTIKA 18 , 1890 


(PART 1 


So it cannot be said by any stretch of imagination that on the date of the termination of 
his services , the bank knew that he had attained the age of 18 years because this was made 
known to the bank in August, 1966 . So even judged from that angle , the order of termi 
pation of the services of the workman made by the bank cannot be held to be mala fide. 

6 . The learned representative of the workman vchemently argued that the order of 
termination of the services of the workman made by thc bank , even if he was below 18 
at the time of his appointment, contravened the provisions of Para . 139 of the All India 
Industrial Tribunal ( Bank Disputes ), award commonly known as the Sastry Award . Para . 
139 of the said award is contained in Chapter VII of the award and prcscribes the scales 
of pay for subordinate staff. It does not laydown the conditions of cligibility to the 
appointment in th > service of the bank . It lays down , inter alia , that an employce of the 
bank if he happens tu be below 18 years of age will get Rs, 5 / - less than the other em 
ployees who are above 18 years . It clocs no liy clown tirat a person below the age of 18 
veais should necessarily bę cnirijve with the bank if the terms and conditions of employ 
ment of that particular bank de riot so wur. nt Shri M . K , Jain , Assistant Manager of 
the bank has produced a copy of the circular datel 6th of March , 1957 issued by the bank 
in which it is categorically state ! that the minimum age of an employee at the time of his 
appointment shoull not be less than 13 yu5 except in cee of cashiers staff whose 
minimum age limit was fixed at 21 years. , therefore , do not think that para . 139 of the 
Sastry Award , in any way , over -rides this rule the bank which relates to the recruit 
ment of peons, daftries , clerical staff cte. Shri Dhurmvir as WW1 himself admitted that 
at the time of application his age was not 19 years. I, therefore , fecl that para . 139 of the 
Sastry Award is not upposed to the order mille by the bank which was in pursuance of 
their recruitment rules . 

7 . It was next contended that the procedure for tcrmination of employment and taking - 
disciplinary action as laid down in Chapter XVIII of the Desai Award WIS not followed 
in the prezent case and for that reason the order of termination was bad in law . It is true 
that the workman was not change -shceteul nor way he served with any notice before the 
order of termination of his servi . cs Wits pa . . The learned representative of the bank 
frankly conccded that even if thi, wns a lacuna , the entire matter was before this Tribunal 
and it was to be scco by it, if lhe onder was justified or not. There are authorities on this 
point that if a refcrence is made to the Tribunal about the wrong termination of services 
of an employce and the whole matter is before it, then it can adjudichte even if there has 
been no cnquiry 25 2 result of the charge - sheet against the deliquent employee . This being 
80 , I have given my very careful consideration to the dispute between the parties in the 
present adjudication . The point is quite simple . The employce wrongly stated in his 
application that he was 19 years of age at the time of his appointment though according 
to his own admission he was not. The bank after a few months detected that the appoint 
ment was wrong as according to their rules , which admit of no exception , a minor had 
been aprointed . At the time of termination of his services, the bank even did not know 
that he had come of age. In August when it was made known to it , that the dato of birth 
of the workman way 20th of June, 1948 and that he had come of age by that time, it again 
took him in cmployment with effect from 21st of September, 1966 . The prayer of the 
workman is that thự period which elapsed between the termination of service anil his fresh 
appointment should be condoned and he should be treated to have continued in service , 
In my opinion , the bank was perfectly justified after the letection of the mistake in termi 
nating the service of the workman and there was no mala fiite or bad intention in its order . 
Even before me from the statement of the workmin no such inference can be drawn . His 
vague statement that the m3nager of thc Khandwa branch of the bank wanted to appoint 
his own man in his place is not horne out from way documentary evidence on the record , 
1, therefore , do not feel that the order was mala fide and without justification and the work 
man is entitled to any relieſ. The award is made accordingly ,

New Delhi, the 31st October 1968 
S . O . 3963. - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 53 of the Banking 
Regulation Act, 1949 ( 10 of 1949), the Central Government, on the recommendation of 
the Reserve Bank of India , hereby declares that the provisions of section 9 of the said 
Act shall not apply to the Central Bank of India Ltd ., Bombay, in respect of the property , 
viz ., Taj building held by it in Bombay, till the 15th March 1968 . 

[No. F . 15(6 ) -BC /68 .] 
V , SWAMINATHAN , Under Secy .
